# Spec revision 41: what changed in the records

Generated by `tools/record_changes.py`; do not edit. Each generated record as committed at
`1ca9bb4` (2026-10-28: the records of revision 39's regeneration, which revision 40 left as they were),
against the record after revision 41's one regeneration (the stage 3 milestone review's
triage batch, 2026-10-28: T-131 (b), T-133 (b), T-145 (a), T-146, T-147, T-149), read at commit
`c9b9b0c`, the last before revision 43's regeneration moved the records; both sides are read from git,
so this page is frozen. Revision 41 changed rules, so
a changed verdict here is a **new result under a new rule**, reported beside revision 40's and version 1's
verdicts and never in their place (`M11.F`); a premise passing is never a finding (`M11.5`). It is not a
defect. A row's verdict is its outcome (a criterion, or a criterion under a sweep setting) or whether the
criterion caught the mutant (a mutation row). An effect **moved** when its mean difference changed by more than
the half-width revision 40's record stated; moved effects are listed only for rows whose verdict held. "Differ at
all" counts every field of a row on both sides that is not exactly revision 40's (the verdict, the seed count, the
flags, each readout's mean difference, half-width and p), including a field on one side only; each is listed
under its record, and "No change" means none differs. A record's hash line must differ from revision 40's, or the
tool refuses: an unchanged hash means it was not regenerated. A record with no row on both sides is refused too.

| Record | Hash at revision 40 | Hash now | Rows compared | Verdicts changed | Effects moved | Differ at all | Only at revision 40 | Only now |

| `docs/phase_d_ensemble_record.md` | `042166ee2147` | `c3da3729d3cf` | 25 | 4 | 0 | 247 | 0 | 0 |

In the level-occupancy record, which has no verdicts, "Verdicts changed" counts the rows whose values changed.

**Not compared here:**

- `docs/phase_c_diagnostic_record.md`: it has no machine-readable block; `git diff 1ca9bb4 -- docs/phase_c_diagnostic_record.md` shows what changed.
- `docs/review/phase_c_trace_seed7.md, docs/review/phase_c_chart_seed7.html`: one seed's run, rendered; S1.7a adds lock-in release rows by design, so `git diff 1ca9bb4` on them shows that and any change of trajectory.
- `docs/review/phase_b_trace_seed7.md, docs/review/phase_b_trace_seed7_nadia.md`: Phase B's seed-7 traces, rendered at Phase B's close (`3132c69`) and not regenerated since, so they are revision 40's.
- `docs/phase_c_completion_report.md §11`: generated from the Phase C records compared here (`tools/c16_report.py`).
- `docs/spec_coverage.md`: generated from the tests and the ensemble and mutation records compared here.
- `docs/phase_b_mutation_record.md`: the record of Phase B's close at commit `3132c69`, frozen (plan D5) and not regenerated, so it is revision 40's apart from the status line stage 1 added; `tools/mutation_gate.py` refuses to rewrite it.
- `docs/version_2_sweep_record.md, docs/version_2_status.md`: the status page is generated from the records compared here (`tools/version_2_status.py`); the sweep record is rendered from its own cache (`tools/version_2_sweep.py`) and was rerun on revision 41's engine by the stage-end sweep, split across both Macs (T-159), so its results are not revision 40's; `git diff 1ca9bb4` on it shows each criterion's held fraction, or its refusal at the rerun (`M17.A.1`), then and now.
- `docs/review/group_g_readout.md`: revision 36's before-and-after measurement on 20 seeds (`tools/group_g_readout.py`): its "after" section is regenerated on each later engine, so `git diff 1ca9bb4` on it shows what revision 41 moved.
- `docs/review/stage_3_projection_trace.md, docs/review/stage_3_iposition_probe.md, docs/review/stage_3_t130_event_sizes.md, docs/review/stage_3_displaced_coping_probe.md`: stage 3's measurements before any rule changed, kept as measured on their steps' engines and not regenerated, so they are revision 40's.
- `docs/review/stage_3_unit_mutation_gate.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_7_criteria.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_7_c2_mutants.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_8_criteria.md, docs/review/stage_3_step_9_criteria.md`: stage 3's measurements between the open batch's commits (D31), kept as measured on their steps' engines and not regenerated, so they are revision 40's.
- `docs/version_1_status.md`: version 1's status page, frozen at the tag: `tests/test_version_1_status.py` checks that it is the tag's, byte for byte.
- `docs/review/phase_c_famdiag_seed7.json`: the seed-7 run as a family diagram (`tools/famdiag_export.py`); regenerated by revision 41's batch (`tools/famdiag_export.py --seed 7`), so `git diff 1ca9bb4` on it shows the change of trajectory under revision 41's rules.
